config/cluster.toml Reference cluster config: nodes, partitions, QoS, accounts.
config/scheduler.toml Scheduler tuning: backfill depth, pass interval, priority weights, half-life.
hpcsched-core/Cargo.toml Crate manifest; no tokio dependency.
hpcsched-core/src/lib.rs Re-exports all public core types.
hpcsched-core/src/job.rs Job, JobId, StepId, JobState FSM, ResourceSpec.
hpcsched-core/src/resource.rs ResourceSpec fields, validation helpers (nodes/cpus/mem/gpus/time bounds).
hpcsched-core/src/node.rs Node, NodeId, NodeState, CoreBitmap, GpuSet.
hpcsched-core/src/partition.rs Partition, partition validation helpers.
hpcsched-core/src/allocation.rs Allocation type, CoreBitmap/GpuSet field layout.
hpcsched-core/src/reservation.rs Reservation type: window, node_set, job_id.
hpcsched-core/src/priority.rs PriorityFactors, priority scalar computation signature.
hpcsched-core/src/account.rs Account, Association, TresLimits types (fair-share tree leaves).
hpcsched-core/src/tres.rs TRES dimension enum, TRES-seconds accrual type.
hpcsched-core/src/error.rs CoreError enum; shared error vocabulary.
hpcsched-proto/Cargo.toml Crate manifest; depends on serde, bincode/prost.
hpcsched-proto/src/lib.rs Re-exports all message types.
hpcsched-proto/src/messages.rs SubmitReq/Resp, AllocMsg, LaunchMsg, HeartbeatMsg, StatusMsg, CancelReq/Resp.
hpcsched-proto/src/codec.rs Length-prefixed frame encoder/decoder over TCP.
hpcsched-proto/src/wire.rs Serialisation round-trip tests.
hpcsched-controld/Cargo.toml Crate manifest; tokio, clap, crate deps.
hpcsched-controld/src/main.rs Binary entry point: config load, tracing init, daemon start.
hpcsched-controld/src/daemon.rs Top-level event loop; RPC listener; tick scheduler; node-heartbeat monitor.
hpcsched-controld/src/state.rs In-memory authoritative state: job table, node table, partition table, allocation index.
hpcsched-controld/src/rpc.rs RPC handler dispatch: submit, cancel, status, sinfo, squeue queries.
hpcsched-controld/src/node_manager.rs Node registration, heartbeat tracking, timeout-driven Down transitions.
hpcsched-controld/src/sched_driver.rs Invokes hpcsched-sched::run_pass() each scheduler tick; feeds queue/node snapshots; commits SchedulePassResult to state.rs + WAL.
hpcsched-controld/src/config.rs cluster.toml + scheduler.toml parsing into typed structs.
hpcsched-noded/Cargo.toml Crate manifest; tokio, nix, cgroups-rs.
hpcsched-noded/src/main.rs Binary entry: register with controld, start heartbeat task, listen for launch.
hpcsched-noded/src/launcher.rs Spawn job step processes; apply cgroup; capture PID and exit code.
hpcsched-noded/src/cgroup.rs Create/configure/teardown cgroup hierarchy per step.
hpcsched-noded/src/monitor.rs Poll running steps; detect exit; send StatusMsg to controld.
hpcsched-noded/src/heartbeat.rs Periodic HeartbeatMsg construction and send; heartbeat interval timer task.
hpcsched-noded/src/resource_report.rs Gather node CPU/mem/GPU inventory; populate HeartbeatMsg.
hpcsched-queue/Cargo.toml Crate manifest; pure logic, no async.
hpcsched-queue/src/lib.rs PendingQueue: priority-ordered insertion, pop, peek, eligible-set view.
hpcsched-queue/src/partition_view.rs Per-partition filtered views; partition limit enforcement.
hpcsched-queue/src/eligible.rs Eligible-set computation: filter by QoS limits, association limits, hold flags.
hpcsched-alloc/Cargo.toml Crate manifest; depends on hpcsched-core.
hpcsched-alloc/src/lib.rs allocate() / free() entry points.
hpcsched-alloc/src/bitmap.rs CoreBitmap / GpuSet transactional ops: try_set, clear, popcount.
hpcsched-alloc/src/selector.rs Node selection: feature matching, topology affinity, best-fit for fragmentation.
hpcsched-alloc/src/mem.rs Per-node memory accounting: allocate / release slabs.
hpcsched-sched/Cargo.toml Crate manifest; depends on queue, alloc, fairshare.
hpcsched-sched/src/lib.rs SchedulePass, run_pass() signature; re-exports.
hpcsched-sched/src/main_pass.rs Priority-ordered allocation loop; Configuring transition.
hpcsched-sched/src/backfill.rs EASY backfill: reservation computation, gap search, BackfillPlan construction.
hpcsched-sched/src/reservation.rs Reservation create/update/expire; time-advance simulation.
hpcsched-sched/src/preempt.rs Preemption candidate selection; suspend/requeue dispatch to controld.
hpcsched-sched/src/gang.rs Gang time-slice coordination: cgroup-freezer tick, cross-node co-scheduling.
hpcsched-fairshare/Cargo.toml Crate manifest; pure computation.
hpcsched-fairshare/src/lib.rs Fair-share tree traversal; priority scalar computation entry point.
hpcsched-fairshare/src/tree.rs Account / Association tree: insert, lookup, rollup.
hpcsched-fairshare/src/decay.rs Half-life decay application; periodic rollup of usage from accounting.
hpcsched-fairshare/src/factors.rs PriorityFactors → u32 multifactor formula; weight normalization.
hpcsched-acct/Cargo.toml Crate manifest; depends on core, state.
hpcsched-acct/src/lib.rs Accounting entry points: record start, record end, query.
hpcsched-acct/src/record.rs JobAcctRecord, TresUsage types (I10 accrual fields).
hpcsched-acct/src/store.rs Append-only on-disk accounting store; write path invoked at job terminal state.
hpcsched-acct/src/tres_accrual.rs TRES-seconds increment on job completion; rounding invariant.
hpcsched-acct/src/limits.rs Association + QoS limit checks at submit time and scheduling time.
hpcsched-acct/src/query.rs sacct-equivalent query: filter by user, account, time range, job state.
hpcsched-state/Cargo.toml Crate manifest; depends on core, serde, storage backend.
hpcsched-state/src/lib.rs StateLog, Checkpoint types; open/replay/append/snapshot API.
hpcsched-state/src/record.rs WalRecord enum: JobSubmitted, JobAllocated, JobState, NodeState, CheckpointRef variants.
hpcsched-state/src/wal.rs WAL append, CRC framing, sequential read for replay.
hpcsched-state/src/checkpoint.rs Snapshot serialise/deserialise; atomic write via temp + rename.
hpcsched-state/src/replay.rs WAL replay engine: apply records to in-memory state; idempotency guard.
hpcsched-cli/Cargo.toml Crate manifest; clap, depends on proto, core.
hpcsched-cli/src/main.rs Shared CLI harness: RPC client setup, common clap argument groups, error formatting — used by each src/bin/*.rs binary.
hpcsched-cli/src/output.rs Column formatting, JSON mode, human-readable TRES display.
hpcsched-cli/src/bin/sbatch.rs Job submission binary: parse PBS/SLURM directives, send SubmitReq, print JobId.
hpcsched-cli/src/bin/srun.rs Interactive step launch binary: allocate-or-attach, send LaunchMsg, forward stdio.
hpcsched-cli/src/bin/squeue.rs Query pending/running jobs binary; tabular output with configurable fields.
hpcsched-cli/src/bin/scancel.rs Send CancelReq binary; support pattern cancel (user, partition, state).
hpcsched-cli/src/bin/sinfo.rs Query node and partition state binary; report NodeState, avail/total TRES.
hpcsched-cli/src/bin/sacct.rs Query job accounting records binary; filter flags; TRES-seconds output.
tests/integration/submit_and_run.rs End-to-end: submit job → Running → Completed on embedded cluster.
tests/integration/backfill.rs Fixture: top-job reservation held; lower job backfills; top job starts on time.
tests/integration/fairshare.rs Fixture: two users with different historical usage; verify priority ordering.
tests/integration/preemption.rs Fixture: high-QoS job preempts running low-QoS job; lower job requeued.
tests/integration/node_failure.rs Fixture: node heartbeat timeout → Down; running jobs requeued; scheduling resumes.
tests/integration/failover.rs Fixture: kill primary controller mid-allocation; standby promotes; no duplicate allocation.
tests/integration/accounting_limits.rs Fixture: GrpTRES limit blocks excess submissions; sacct records accurate TRES-seconds.
tests/conformance/slurm_semantics.rs Declarative conformance table: known Slurm behaviours encoded as pass/fail fixtures.
tests/fuzz/protocol_fuzz.rs cargo-fuzz harness: arbitrary bytes decoded as proto messages; no panic.
benches/scheduling_pass.rs Criterion benchmark: scheduling pass over 10k pending jobs; latency target (M1).
benches/backfill_depth.rs Benchmark: backfill scan over varying depth; verify bounded latency (M2).
benches/bitmap_alloc.rs Criterion benchmark: CoreBitmap set/clear throughput (M5).
benches/fairshare_recompute.rs Criterion benchmark: priority recompute over 10k-job queue (M6).
benches/wal_append.rs Criterion benchmark: WAL append throughput, fsync vs. fdatasync (M3).

Appendix B — Milestone Summary

Durations are calendar-week estimates; sum = 40 weeks (Phase 0 is pre-work overlap with Phase 1).

Phase Deliverable Duration Key Risks Exit Criteria
0 — Setup Workspace, CI, lint, hpcsched-core skeleton, ADR log 1 w Topology of crate deps settled incorrectly cargo check clean; core types compile; CI green
1 — Core model + controller skeleton Job/Node/ResourceSpec/Partition types; JobState FSM; in-memory controller state; event loop; config parse 2 w FSM gaps discovered late; config schema churn All FSM transitions covered by unit tests; scheduler tick fires; nodes loadable from cluster.toml
2 — Control-plane RPC + node daemon hpcsched-proto messages; noded register + heartbeat; live node inventory; heartbeat-timeout Down 2 w Codec framing bugs; TLS/auth scope creep Two noded instances register; heartbeat timeout sets node Down; integration test passes
3 — Submission + pending queue + durable state sbatch → submit RPC → Pending; WAL append; controller restart replays to exact prior state 3 w WAL corruption handling; idempotency edge cases 1 000-job replay test; restart loses zero committed jobs; duplicate-submit rejected
4 — Resource allocation engine CoreBitmap/GpuSet/mem; allocate(spec); transactional set-bits; free on completion 2 w Fragmentation under mixed GPU/CPU workloads; race on concurrent alloc calls I1/I2 invariants hold under property tests; allocate + free round-trips verified by quickcheck
5 — Priority scheduling loop Main pass over pending queue; Pending→Configuring; partition limits; static priority + tiebreak 2 w Priority inversion; partition limit not enforced atomically Correct ordering fixture; partition max-nodes respected; pass latency ≤ target on 1k jobs
6 — Job launch + steps + lifecycle LaunchMsg → noded spawns; state Configuring→Running→Completing→Completed; epilog; exit-code capture; srun 3 w Process leak on noded crash; epilog hang blocks scheduling Full lifecycle integration test; exit code propagated; epilog timeout kills step
7 — EASY backfill Reservation for top pending job; backfill lower-priority jobs in gaps; I4 invariant 3 w Inaccurate time-limit estimates cause delayed top job; O(n²) scan cost Conformance fixture: top job never delayed; backfill throughput benchmark passes depth limit
8 — Multifactor priority + fair-share Hierarchical association tree; decayed usage; PriorityFactors → u32; recompute each pass 2 w Decay arithmetic overflow/underflow; tree rollup cost on deep hierarchies Fair-share fixture: heavy user yields after period; ranking reproducible from same state (P8)
9 — Preemption + gang + QoS Higher-QoS preempts lower; gang time-slice; QoS limits + preempt modes; grace period 3 w Preemption storm; gang-scheduler timer drift; double-cancel on concurrent preempt Preemption conformance fixture; grace-period respected; gang-sliced jobs complete without starvation
10 — Node fault handling Heartbeat-timeout → Down; running jobs requeued or Failed; drain; node return reconciles 2 w Requeue after partial epilog; split-brain if heartbeat delayed not lost I6 invariant holds; node-failure integration test; drain drains without killing running jobs
11 — Accounting + limits TRES-seconds records; association/QoS limits at submit + schedule; sacct query; usage feeds fairshare 2 w TRES-seconds rounding across partial periods; limit check TOCTOU I10 invariant verified; GrpTRES blocks excess jobs; sacct output matches expected records
12 — Controller HA / failover Primary WAL+checkpoint to shared store; standby tails; failover promotes; fencing; no double-alloc 3 w Fencing race; standby lag causes stale state promotion I5/I9 invariants hold across kill-primary test; zero duplicate allocations across 100 failover cycles
13 — CLI suite + observability squeue, sinfo, scancel, sacct binaries; metrics (queue depth, utilisation, backfill eff., pass latency) 2 w Output format compatibility; metric cardinality explosion All CLI binaries produce correct tabular output; Prometheus metrics scraped; dashboard fixture
14 — Scale + soak + conformance 10k+ job scheduling pass under latency target; soak with churn; protocol fuzz; conformance suite 3 w Regression at scale breaks earlier invariants; fuzz finds parser panic Scheduling pass ≤ target latency at 10k jobs; 72h soak clean; fuzz corpus 10k inputs, no panic; conformance suite 100% pass
